Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be `>=` the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`.\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its three indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane and all derived data stores against PostgreSQL before exiting `0`:\n    - All unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) must be relayed to SQS.\n    - The DynamoDB projection table must contain **only** partitions for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements`, and each partition `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` must contain **only** `SK = \"STATE\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#00000001\" .. \"EVENT#<version:08d>\"` with every attribute (including `AccountIndex` `GSI1PK` / `GSI1SK`, `updated_at`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, and each event's `entry_id`, `memo`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, and `envelope`) matching PostgreSQL.\n    - Valkey (`clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`) must not retain orphan keys, entries without a valid TTL in `(0, 90]`, or cached `SettlementProjection` JSON payloads that diverge from the authoritative projection state.\n    - The S3 audit archive bucket (`s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>`) must contain objects **only** under `ledger-audit/`, every object key and body must conform to `ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson` with records ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, the union of all S3 batch records must match `clearledger.outbox` **1-to-1** (every committed outbox event present in S3 **exactly once** with its exact canonical JSON `payload`, and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL), and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) must be permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each canonical batch remains.\n    - Any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu, as well as any unrestricted `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` egress rules on the `alb`, `rds`, or `valkey` security groups, must be removed during `deploy.sh` convergence.\n- **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n  - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including customer-managed KMS keys scheduled for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n- **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n\n/workspace/evidence:\ntotal 0\ndrwxr-xr-x 2 agent agent  6 Oct  9 18:49 .\ndrwxr-xr-x 6 agent agent 91 Oct  9 18:50 ..\n\n/workspace/submission:\ntotal 0\ndrwxr-xr-x 3 agent agent 19 Oct  9 18:50 .\ndrwxr-xr-x 6 agent agent 91 Oct  9 18:50 ..\ndrwxr-xr-x 2 agent agent  6 Oct  9 18:49 infra\n","metadata":{"output":"# ClearLedger Infrastructure Contract\n\nProvision the ClearLedger clearing and settlement platform using Terraform or OpenTofu in `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state stored at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n\n## Required Input Source\n\nRead runtime inputs from `/workspace/config/config.json`:\n\n- `resource_prefix`: unique prefix for this deployment (for example `cl-a1b2c3`)\n- `region`: AWS region (`us-east-1`)\n- `aws_endpoint_url`: AWS control-plane endpoint (`http://aws:4566`)\n- `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`: PostgreSQL database name and credentials\n- `api_image`, `projector_image`, `relay_image`, `archiver_image`: container image references\n- `api_image_id`, `projector_image_id`, `relay_image_id`, `archiver_image_id`: image digest identifiers\n\nEvery taggable AWS resource created by your configuration must carry the tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` (at least 25 tagged resources) and have its name or identifier prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`. Must be executable and idempotent (timeout: 720s).\n   - Runs `terraform` or `tofu` against `/workspace/submission/infra` with state saved at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n   - Idempotently initializes the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md).\n   - Writes `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) conforming strictly to `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.\n   - Polls `GET <service_url>/health/ready` until the API returns HTTP `200`.\n   - Re-running `deploy.sh` must preserve the RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket without data loss, reconcile control-plane drift across all managed AWS resources (including SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge Scheduler schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda worker environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles, and security group egress rules), recreate any missing resources or event source mappings, and converge all derived data stores (DynamoDB, Valkey, and the versioned S3 audit bucket including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) 1-to-1 with authoritative PostgreSQL state before exiting `0`.\n2. `/workspace/submission/destroy.sh`:\n   - \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, and attributes `settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `version` (`N`), `entry_count` (`N - 1`), `updated_at` (RFC3339 timestamp of the latest event), and optional `last_entry_id` / `last_memo`.\n- Ordered ledger event items with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"` (`EVENT#00000001` .. `EVENT#<version:08d>`) containing `settlement_id`, `event_id`, `version` (`N`), `event_type`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, `envelope`, and optional `entry_id` / `memo`.\n\nBecause `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, `deploy.sh` must ensure full 1-to-1 convergence between PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events`) and DynamoDB/Valkey so that no orphan partitions, stray sort keys, missing event items, or stale/divergent attributes remain in either DynamoDB or Valkey.\n\n# ElastiCache for Valkey `clearledger.outbox.seq`, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy.\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - `clearledger.outbox.seq` order, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly once** across the S3 audit batches with its exact canonical JSON `payload` and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL, and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) are permanently purged from the bucket so only the IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy.\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Read-only access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:Query`, `dynamodb:DescribeTable`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - Idempotent: safe to run on every deploy, also under live traffic.\n-- Existing objects are only touched when they are missing, disabled or have a stale function body.\n\\set ON_ERROR_STOP on\nSET client_min_messages = warning;\nSET lock_timeout = '30s';\n\nBEGIN;\nSELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(727274001);\n\nCREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS clearledger;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Helper functions (CREATE OR REPLACE does not lock the tables that use them)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_uuid(v text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT coalesce(v ~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$', false) $$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.tlen(v text) RETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT char_length(btrim(v)) $$;\n\n-- Rank of the linear clearing lifecycle; DISPUTED has no rank.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_rank(s text) RETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT CASE s\n     WHEN 'INITIATED' THEN 0 WHEN 'VALIDATED' THEN 1 WHEN 'RESERVED' THEN 2\n     WHEN 'CLEARED'   THEN 3 WHEN 'SETTLED'   THEN 4 WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5\n     ELSE NULL END $$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_known(s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT coalesce(s IN ('INITIATED','VALIDATED','RESERVED','CLEARED','SETTLED','RECONCILED','DISPUTED'), false) $$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_transition_ok(old_s text, new_s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT CASE\n     WHEN NOT (clearledger.status_known(old_s) AND clearledger.status_known(new_s)) THEN false\n     WHEN old_s = 'RECONCILED' THEN false\n     WHEN old_s = 'DISPUTED'   THEN new_s IN ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n     WHEN new_s = 'DISPUTED'   THEN true\n     ELSE clearledger.status_rank(new_s) >= clearledger.status_rank(old_s)\n   END $$;\n\n-- Strict validation of ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (closed schema) including the\n-- version / kind / status / entryId coupling. Never raises: malformed input yields false.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.envelope_valid(p jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  d jsonb;\n  v integer;\n  k text;\n  ts_re constant text := '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}([.][0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$';\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(p) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                 'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data') THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (p ?& ARRAY['schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                     'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data']) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'schemaVersion') <> 'string' OR p->>'schemaVersion' <> '1.0' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateType') <> 'string' OR p->>'aggregateType' <> 'settlement' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'eventType') <> 'string' OR p->>'eventType' NOT IN ('SettlementInitiated','LedgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'eventId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid(p->>'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid(p->>'aggregateId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateVersion') <> 'number' OR (p->>'aggregateVersion') !~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'occurredAt') <> 'string' OR (p->>'occurredAt') !~ ts_re THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  PERFORM (p->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'correlationId') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(p->>'correlationId') NOT BETWEEN 4 AND 128 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'idempotencyKey') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(p->>'idempotencyKey') NOT BETWEEN 8 AND 128 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  d := p->'data';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(d) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','entryId','status','clearingStage','memo') THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (d ?& ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','status','clearingStage']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'reference') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'reference') NOT BETWEEN 3 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'debitParty') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'debitParty') NOT BETWEEN 2 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'creditParty') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'creditParty') NOT BETWEEN 2 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'status') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.status_known(d->>'status') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'clearingStage') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'clearingStage') NOT BETWEEN 2 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'memo' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') NOT IN ('null','string') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') = 'string' AND clearledger.tlen(d->>'memo') NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 256 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'entryId' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') NOT IN ('null','string') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') = 'string' AND NOT clearledger.is_uuid(d->>'entryId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF clearledger.tlen(d->>'debitParty') = clearledger.tlen(d->>'creditParty')\n     AND btrim(d->>'debitParty') = btrim(d->>'creditParty') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF v = 1 THEN\n    IF p->>'eventType' <> 'SettlementInitiated' OR d->>'kind' <> 'settlementInitiated'\n       OR d->>'status' <> 'INITIATED' OR jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') = 'string' THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF p->>'eventType' <> 'LedgerEntryRecorded' OR d->>'kind' <> 'ledgerEntryRecorded'\n       OR d->>'status' = 'INITIATED' OR jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') <> 'string' THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN true;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column / envelope equality for clearledger.events rows.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.event_row_matches(\n  p_event_id uuid, p_settlement_id uuid, p_version integer, p_event_type text,\n  p_correlation_id text, p_idempotency_key text, p_occurred_at timestamptz, p_payload jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RETURN clearledger.envelope_valid(p_payload)\n     AND (p_payload->>'eventId')::uuid = p_event_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateId')::uuid = p_settlement_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = p_version\n     AND p_payload->>'eventType' = p_event_type\n     AND p_payload->>'correlationId' = p_correlation_id\n     AND p_payload->>'idempotencyKey' = p_idempotency_key\n     AND (p_payload->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz = p_occurred_at;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column / envelope equality for clearledger.outbox rows.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_row_matches(\n  p_event_id uuid, p_settlement_id uuid, p_version integer, p_correlation_id text, p_payload jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RETURN clearledger.envelope_valid(p_payload)\n     AND (p_payload->>'eventId')::uuid = p_event_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateId')::uuid = p_settlement_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = p_version\n     AND p_payload->>'correlationId' = p_correlation_id;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Closed-schema WriteAcceptedResponse as stored in idempotency_keys.response_body.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.write_accepted_valid(b jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$\nDECLARE k text;\nBEGIN\n  IF b IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(b) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(b) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (b ?& ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  RETURN jsonb_typeof(b->'settlementId') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid(b->>'settlementId')\n     AND jsonb_typeof(b->'eventId') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid(b->>'eventId')\n     AND jsonb_typeof(b->'version') = 'number' AND (b->>'version') ~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$'\n     AND b->'accepted' = 'true'::jsonb\n     AND b->'idempotentReplay' = 'false'::jsonb;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_row_valid(\n  p_scope text, p_key text, p_hash text, p_status integer, p_body jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$\nDECLARE v integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.write_accepted_valid(p_body) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := (p_body->>'version')::integer;\n  RETURN CASE\n    WHEN p_scope ~* '^create:[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$'\n      THEN p_status = 201 AND v = 1\n           AND lower(substr(p_scope, 8)) = lower(p_body->>'settlementId')\n    WHEN p_scope ~* '^entry:[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$'\n      THEN p_status = 202 AND v >= 2\n           AND lower(substr(p_scope, 7)) = lower(p_body->>'settlementId')\n    ELSE false END;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Tables\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.settlements (\n  settlement_id  UUID        NOT NULL,\n  account_id     TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  reference      TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  debit_party    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  credit_party   TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  current_status TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  current_stage  TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  last_entry_id  UUID        NULL,\n  last_memo      TEXT        NULL,\n  version        INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  entry_count    INTEGER     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  created_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  updated_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  CONSTRAINT settlements_pkey PRIMARY KEY (settlement_id)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.events (\n  seq               BIGSERIAL   NOT NULL,\n  event_id          UUID        NOT NULL,\n  settlement_id     UUID        NOT NULL,\n  aggregate_version INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  event_type        TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  correlation_id    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  idempotency_key   TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  occurred_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n  payload           JSONB       NOT NULL,\n  created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  CONSTRAINT events_pkey PRIMARY KEY (seq)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.outbox (\n  seq               BIGSERIAL   NOT NULL,\n  event_id          UUID        NOT NULL,\n  settlement_id     UUID        NOT NULL,\n  aggregate_version INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  correlation_id    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  payload           JSONB       NOT NULL,\n  created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  published_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n  archived_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n  attempts          INTEGER     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  last_error        TEXT        NULL,\n  CONSTRAINT outbox_pkey PRIMARY KEY (seq)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clearledger.idempotency_keys (\n  scope           TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  idempotency_key TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  request_hash    TEXT        NOT NULL,\n  status_code     INTEGER     NOT NULL,\n  response_body   JSONB       NOT NULL,\n  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n  CONSTRAINT idempotency_keys_pkey PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)\n);\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Constraints (added only when missing)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE TEMP TABLE _cl_constraints (tbl text, name text, ddl text) ON COMMIT DROP;\nINSERT INTO _cl_constraints VALUES\n  -- settlements\n  ('settlements','settlements_account_id_len',   'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(account_id) BETWEEN 3 AND 64)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_reference_len',    'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(reference) BETWEEN 3 AND 64)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_debit_party_len',  'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(debit_party) BETWEEN 2 AND 64)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_credit_party_len', 'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(credit_party) BETWEEN 2 AND 64)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_parties_differ',   'CHECK (btrim(debit_party) <> btrim(credit_party))'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_status_valid',     'CHECK (current_status IN (''INITIATED'',''VALIDATED'',''RESERVED'',''CLEARED'',''SETTLED'',''RECONCILED'',''DISPUTED''))'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_stage_len',        'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(current_stage) BETWEEN 2 AND 64)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_memo_len',         'CHECK (last_memo IS NULL OR clearledger.tlen(last_memo) BETWEEN 1 AND 256)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_version_positive',  'CHECK (version >= 1)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_entry_count_nonneg','CHECK (entry_count >= 0)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_timestamps_monotonic','CHECK (updated_at >= created_at)'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_initial_state',    'CHECK (version <> 1 OR (entry_count = 0 AND current_status = ''INITIATED'' AND last_entry_id IS NULL AND updated_at = created_at))'),\n  ('settlements','settlements_entry_state',      'CHECK (version = 1 OR (entry_count = version - 1 AND current_status <> ''INITIATED'' AND last_entry_id IS NOT NULL))'),\n  -- events\n  ('events','events_event_id_key',               'UNIQUE (event_id)'),\n  ('events','events_settlement_fkey',            'FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id) REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE'),\n  ('events','events_settlement_version_key',     'UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)'),\n  ('events','events_settlement_idempotency_key', 'UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)'),\n  ('events','events_version_positive',           'CHECK (aggregate_version >= 1)'),\n  ('events','events_event_type_valid',           'CHECK (event_type IN (''SettlementInitiated'',''LedgerEntryRecorded''))'),\n  ('events','events_correlation_id_len',         'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(correlation_id) BETWEEN 4 AND 128)'),\n  ('events','events_idempotency_key_len',        'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(idempotency_key) BETWEEN 8 AND 128)'),\n  ('events','events_payload_envelope',           'CHECK (clearledger.event_row_matches(event_id, settlement_id, aggregate_version, event_type, correlation_id, idempotency_key, occurred_at, payload))'),\n  -- outbox\n  ('outbox','outbox_event_id_key',               'UNIQUE (event_id)'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_event_fkey',                 'FOREIGN KEY (event_id) REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_settlement_fkey',            'FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id) REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_settlement_version_key',     'UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_settlement_version_fkey',    'FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_version_positive',           'CHECK (aggregate_version >= 1)'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_correlation_id_len',         'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(correlation_id) BETWEEN 4 AND 128)'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_payload_envelope',           'CHECK (clearledger.outbox_row_matches(event_id, settlement_id, aggregate_version, correlation_id, payload))'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_attempts_nonneg',            'CHECK (attempts >= 0)'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_published_lifecycle',        'CHECK (published_at IS NULL OR (attempts >= 1 AND last_error IS NULL AND published_at >= created_at))'),\n  ('outbox','outbox_archived_lifecycle',         'CHECK (archived_at IS NULL OR (published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at >= published_at))'),\n  -- idempotency_keys\n  ('idempotency_keys','idempotency_keys_key_len',   'CHECK (clearledger.tlen(idempotency_key) BETWEEN 8 AND 128)'),\n  ('idempotency_keys','idempotency_keys_hash_hex',  'CHECK (request_hash ~ ''^[0-9a-f]{64}$'')'),\n  ('idempotency_keys','idempotency_keys_row_valid', 'CHECK (clearledger.idempotency_row_valid(scope, idempotency_key, request_hash, status_code, response_body))');\n\nDO $$\nDECLARE r record;\nBEGIN\n  FOR r IN SELECT * FROM _cl_constraints LOOP\n    IF NOT EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint c\n      WHERE c.conrelid = format('clearledger.%I', r.tbl)::regclass AND c.conname = r.name\n    ) THEN\n      EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE clearledger.%I ADD CONSTRAINT %I %s', r.tbl, r.name, r.ddl);\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Indexes\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished\n  ON clearledger.outbox (seq) WHERE published_at IS NULL;\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived\n  ON clearledger.outbox (seq) WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL;\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version\n  ON clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version);\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_before_update() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED and can no longer be updated', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.settlement_id <> OLD.settlement_id OR NEW.account_id <> OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference <> OLD.reference OR NEW.debit_party <> OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party <> OLD.credit_party OR NEW.created_at <> OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % header columns are immutable', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version <> OLD.version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % version must step from % to %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.version, OLD.version + 1;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.entry_count <> OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % entry_count must step by one', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % update must carry a new last_entry_id', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at < OLD.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must not move backwards', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % illegal status transition % -> %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.events_before_insert() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  d jsonb := NEW.payload->'data';\n  max_version integer;\n  prev clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % references unknown settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % aggregate_version must be contiguous (expected %, got %)',\n      NEW.settlement_id, coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1, NEW.aggregate_version;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF d->>'kind' = 'ledgerEntryRecorded' AND EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n       WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n         AND e.payload->'data'->>'entryId' = d->>'entryId') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', d->>'entryId', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF s.account_id <> d->>'accountId' OR s.reference <> d->>'reference'\n     OR s.debit_party <> d->>'debitParty' OR s.credit_party <> d->>'creditParty' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % header does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.current_status <> d->>'status' OR s.current_stage <> d->>'clearingStage' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % status/stage does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM (d->>'entryId')::uuid THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % entryId does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.last_memo IS DISTINCT FROM (d->>'memo') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % memo does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.version <> NEW.aggregate_version OR NEW.occurred_at <> s.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % version/occurred_at does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 THEN\n    IF NEW.occurred_at <> s.created_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'initial event % occurred_at must equal settlement created_at', NEW.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    SELECT * INTO prev FROM clearledger.events\n     WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is missing version %', NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.occurred_at < prev.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % occurred_at precedes version %', NEW.event_id, prev.aggregate_version;\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % illegal status transition % -> %', NEW.event_id, prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.events_append_only() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.events is append-only (% rejected)', TG_OP;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_insert() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  e clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  max_version integer;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO e FROM clearledger.events WHERE event_id = NEW.event_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row references unknown event %', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF e.settlement_id <> NEW.settlement_id OR e.aggregate_version <> NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR e.correlation_id <> NEW.correlation_id OR e.payload <> NEW.payload THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row for event % does not mirror clearledger.events', NEW.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox aggregate_version for settlement % must be contiguous (expected %, got %)',\n      NEW.settlement_id, coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1, NEW.aggregate_version;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_update() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.seq <> OLD.seq OR NEW.event_id <> OLD.event_id OR NEW.settlement_id <> OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version <> OLD.aggregate_version OR NEW.correlation_id <> OLD.correlation_id\n     OR NEW.payload <> OLD.payload OR NEW.created_at <> OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable (event %)', OLD.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease (event %)', OLD.event_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- publishing\n    IF NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts OR NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing outbox event % requires attempts to increment and archived_at to stay NULL', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- archival / re-archival\n    IF NEW.published_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.published_at OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published_at, attempts and last_error of published outbox event % are immutable', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at <> OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at of outbox event % must be reset to NULL before it is changed', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay: both markers are reset together\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'replaying outbox event % requires archived_at to be reset as well', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_no_delete() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted';\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_before_insert() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  ev uuid;\n  sid uuid;\n  ver integer;\nBEGIN\n  ev  := (NEW.response_body->>'eventId')::uuid;\n  sid := (NEW.response_body->>'settlementId')::uuid;\n  ver := (NEW.response_body->>'version')::integer;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n                 WHERE e.event_id = ev AND e.settlement_id = sid AND e.aggregate_version = ver\n                   AND e.idempotency_key = NEW.idempotency_key) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference a committed event', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.outbox o\n                 WHERE o.event_id = ev AND o.settlement_id = sid AND o.aggregate_version = ver) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference an outbox row', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_immutable() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.idempotency_keys is immutable (% rejected)', TG_OP;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Triggers (created when missing, re-enabled when disabled out of band)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE TEMP TABLE _cl_triggers (tbl text, name text, ddl text) ON COMMIT DROP;\nINSERT INTO _cl_triggers VALUES\n  ('settlements','trg_settlements_before_update',  'BEFORE UPDATE ON clearledger.settlements FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_before_update()'),\n  ('events','trg_events_before_insert',            'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.events FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.events_before_insert()'),\n  ('events','trg_events_append_only',              'BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.events FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.events_append_only()'),\n  ('outbox','trg_outbox_before_insert',            'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_insert()'),\n  ('outbox','trg_outbox_before_update',            'BEFORE UPDATE ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_update()'),\n  ('outbox','trg_outbox_no_delete',                'BEFORE DELETE ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_no_delete()'),\n  ('idempotency_keys','trg_idempotency_before_insert', 'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.idempotency_keys FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_before_insert()'),\n  ('idempotency_keys','trg_idempotency_immutable', 'BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.idempotency_keys FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION Idempotent: safe to run on every deploy, also under live traffic.\n-- Existing objects are only touched when they are missing, disabled or have a stale function body.\n\\set ON_ERROR_STOP on\nSET client_min_messages = warning;\nSET lock_timeout = '30s';\n\nBEGIN;\nSELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(727274001);\n\nCREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS clearledger;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Helper functions (CREATE OR REPLACE does not lock the tables that use them)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_uuid(v text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT coalesce(v ~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$', false) $$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.tlen(v text) RETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT char_length(btrim(v)) $$;\n\n-- Rank of the linear clearing lifecycle; DISPUTED has no rank.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_rank(s text) RETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT CASE s\n     WHEN 'INITIATED' THEN 0 WHEN 'VALIDATED' THEN 1 WHEN 'RESERVED' THEN 2\n     WHEN 'CLEARED'   THEN 3 WHEN 'SETTLED'   THEN 4 WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5\n     ELSE NULL END $$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_known(s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT coalesce(s IN ('INITIATED','VALIDATED','RESERVED','CLEARED','SETTLED','RECONCILED','DISPUTED'), false) $$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_transition_ok(old_s text, new_s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$ SELECT CASE\n     WHEN NOT (clearledger.status_known(old_s) AND clearledger.status_known(new_s)) THEN false\n     WHEN old_s = 'RECONCILED' THEN false\n     WHEN old_s = 'DISPUTED'   THEN new_s IN ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n     WHEN new_s = 'DISPUTED'   THEN true\n     ELSE clearledger.status_rank(new_s) >= clearledger.status_rank(old_s)\n   END $$;\n\n-- Strict validation of ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (closed schema) including the\n-- version / kind / status / entryId coupling. Never raises: malformed input yields false.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.envelope_valid(p jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  d jsonb;\n  v integer;\n  k text;\n  ts_re constant text := '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}([.][0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$';\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(p) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                 'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data') THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (p ?& ARRAY['schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId','aggregateVersion',\n                     'occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data']) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'schemaVersion') <> 'string' OR p->>'schemaVersion' <> '1.0' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateType') <> 'string' OR p->>'aggregateType' <> 'settlement' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'eventType') <> 'string' OR p->>'eventType' NOT IN ('SettlementInitiated','LedgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'eventId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid(p->>'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateId') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.is_uuid(p->>'aggregateId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateVersion') <> 'number' OR (p->>'aggregateVersion') !~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'occurredAt') <> 'string' OR (p->>'occurredAt') !~ ts_re THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  PERFORM (p->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'correlationId') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(p->>'correlationId') NOT BETWEEN 4 AND 128 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'idempotencyKey') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(p->>'idempotencyKey') NOT BETWEEN 8 AND 128 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  d := p->'data';\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(d) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','entryId','status','clearingStage','memo') THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (d ?& ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','status','clearingStage']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'kind') <> 'string' OR d->>'kind' NOT IN ('settlementInitiated','ledgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'accountId') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'accountId') NOT BETWEEN 3 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'reference') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'reference') NOT BETWEEN 3 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'debitParty') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'debitParty') NOT BETWEEN 2 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'creditParty') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'creditParty') NOT BETWEEN 2 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'status') <> 'string' OR NOT clearledger.status_known(d->>'status') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'clearingStage') <> 'string' OR clearledger.tlen(d->>'clearingStage') NOT BETWEEN 2 AND 64 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'memo' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') NOT IN ('null','string') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') = 'string' AND clearledger.tlen(d->>'memo') NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 256 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'entryId' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') NOT IN ('null','string') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') = 'string' AND NOT clearledger.is_uuid(d->>'entryId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF clearledger.tlen(d->>'debitParty') = clearledger.tlen(d->>'creditParty')\n     AND btrim(d->>'debitParty') = btrim(d->>'creditParty') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF v = 1 THEN\n    IF p->>'eventType' <> 'SettlementInitiated' OR d->>'kind' <> 'settlementInitiated'\n       OR d->>'status' <> 'INITIATED' OR jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') = 'string' THEN\n      RETURN false;\n    END false;\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN true;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column / envelope equality for clearledger.events rows.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.event_row_matches(\n  p_event_id uuid, p_settlement_id uuid, p_version integer, p_event_type text,\n  p_correlation_id text, p_idempotency_key text, p_occurred_at timestamptz, p_payload jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RETURN clearledger.envelope_valid(p_payload)\n     AND (p_payload->>'eventId')::uuid = p_event_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateId')::uuid = p_settlement_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = p_version\n     AND p_payload->>'eventType' = p_event_type\n     AND p_payload->>'correlationId' = p_correlation_id\n     AND p_payload->>'idempotencyKey' = p_idempotency_key\n     AND (p_payload->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz = p_occurred_at;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column / envelope equality for clearledger.outbox rows.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_row_matches(\n  p_event_id uuid, p_settlement_id uuid, p_version integer, p_correlation_id text, p_payload jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RETURN clearledger.envelope_valid(p_payload)\n     AND (p_payload->>'eventId')::uuid = p_event_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateId')::uuid = p_settlement_id\n     AND (p_payload->>'aggregateVersion')::integer = p_version\n     AND p_payload->>'correlationId' = p_correlation_id;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Closed-schema WriteAcceptedResponse as stored in idempotency_keys.response_body.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.write_accepted_valid(b jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$\nDECLARE k text;\nBEGIN\n  IF b IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(b) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(b) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n  IF NOT (b ?& ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  RETURN jsonb_typeof(b->'settlementId') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid(b->>'settlementId')\n     AND jsonb_typeof(b->'eventId') = 'string' AND clearledger.is_uuid(b->>'eventId')\n     AND jsonb_typeof(b->'version') = 'number' AND (b->>'version') ~ '^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$'\n     AND b->'accepted' = 'true'::jsonb\n     AND b->'idempotentReplay' = 'false'::jsonb;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_row_valid(\n  p_scope text, p_key text, p_hash text, p_status integer, p_body jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS\n$$\nDECLARE v integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.write_accepted_valid(p_body) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := (p_body->>'version')::integer;\n  RETURN CASE\n    WHEN p_scope ~* '^create:[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$'\n      THEN p_status = 201 AND v = 1\n           AND lower(substr(p_scope, 8)) = lower(p_body->>'settlementId')\n    WHEN p_scope ~* '^entry:[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$'\n      THEN p_status = 202 AND v >= 2\n           AND lower(substr(p_scope, 7)) = lower(p_body->>'settlementId')\n    ELSE false END;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_before_update() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED and can no longer be updated', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.settlement_id <> OLD.settlement_id OR NEW.account_id <> OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference <> OLD.reference OR NEW.debit_party <> OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party <> OLD.credit_party OR NEW.created_at <> OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % header columns are immutable', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version <> OLD.version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % version must step from % to %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.version, OLD.version + 1;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.entry_count <> OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % entry_count must step by one', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % update must carry a new last_entry_id', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at < OLD.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % updated_at must not move backwards', OLD.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % illegal status transition % -> %', OLD.settlement_id, OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.events_before_insert() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  d jsonb := NEW.payload->'data';\n  max_version integer;\n  prev clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % references unknown settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % aggregate_version must be contiguous (expected %, got %)',\n      NEW.settlement_id, coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1, NEW.aggregate_version;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF d->>'kind' = 'ledgerEntryRecorded' AND EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n       WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n         AND e.payload->'data'->>'entryId' = d->>'entryId') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', d->>'entryId', NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF s.account_id <> d->>'accountId' OR s.reference <> d->>'reference'\n     OR s.debit_party <> d->>'debitParty' OR s.credit_party <> d->>'creditParty' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % header does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.current_status <> d->>'status' OR s.current_stage <> d->>'clearingStage' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % status/stage does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM (d->>'entryId')::uuid THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % entryId does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.last_memo IS DISTINCT FROM (d->>'memo') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % memo does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.version <> NEW.aggregate_version OR NEW.occurred_at <> s.updated_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % version/occurred_at does not match settlement %', NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 THEN\n    IF NEW.occurred_at <> s.created_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'initial event % occurred_at must equal settlement created_at', NEW.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    SELECT * INTO prev FROM clearledger.events\n     WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is missing version %', NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.occurred_at < prev.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % occurred_at precedes version %', NEW.event_id, prev.aggregate_version;\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.status_transition_ok(prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event % illegal status transition % -> %', NEW.event_id, prev.payload->'data'->>'status', d->>'status';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.events_append_only() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.events is append-only (% rejected)', TG_OP;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_insert() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  e clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  max_version integer;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO e FROM clearledger.events WHERE event_id = NEW.event_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row references unknown event %', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF e.settlement_id <> NEW.settlement_id OR e.aggregate_version <> NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR e.correlation_id <> NEW.correlation_id OR e.payload <> NEW.payload THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row for event % does not mirror clearledger.events', NEW.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_version FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version <> coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox aggregate_version for settlement % must be contiguous (expected %, got %)',\n      NEW.settlement_id, coalesce(max_version, 0) + 1, NEW.aggregate_version;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_update() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  IF NEW.seq <> OLD.seq OR NEW.event_id <> OLD.event_id OR NEW.settlement_id <> OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version <> OLD.aggregate_version OR NEW.correlation_id <> OLD.correlation_id\n     OR NEW.payload <> OLD.payload OR NEW.created_at <> OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable (event %)', OLD.event_id;\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease (event %)', OLD.event_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- publishing\n    IF NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts OR NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing outbox event % requires attempts to increment and archived_at to stay NULL', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- archival / re-archival\n    IF NEW.published_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.published_at OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published_at, attempts and last_error of published outbox event % are immutable', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at <> OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at of outbox event % must be reset to NULL before it is changed', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay: both markers are reset together\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'replaying outbox event % requires archived_at to be reset as well', OLD.event_id;\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_no_delete() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted';\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_before_insert() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nDECLARE\n  ev uuid;\n  sid uuid;\n  ver integer;\nBEGIN\n  ev  := (NEW.response_body->>'eventId')::uuid;\n  sid := (NEW.response_body->>'settlementId')::uuid;\n  ver := (NEW.response_body->>'version')::integer;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n                 WHERE e.event_id = ev AND e.settlement_id = sid AND e.aggregate_version = ver\n                   AND e.idempotency_key = NEW.idempotency_key) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference a committed event', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.outbox o\n                 WHERE o.event_id = ev AND o.settlement_id = sid AND o.aggregate_version = ver) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency key % does not reference an outbox row', NEW.idempotency_key USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_immutable() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS\n$$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.idempotency_keys is immutable (% rejected)', TG_OP;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Triggers (created when missing, re-enabled when disabled out of band)\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE TEMP TABLE _cl_triggers (tbl text, name text, ddl text) ON COMMIT DROP;\nINSERT INTO _cl_triggers VALUES\n  ('settlements','trg_settlements_before_update',  'BEFORE UPDATE ON clearledger.settlements FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_before_update()'),\n  ('events','trg_events_before_insert',            'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.events FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.events_before_insert()'),\n  ('events','trg_events_append_only',              'BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.events FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.events_append_only()'),\n  ('outbox','trg_outbox_before_insert',            'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_insert()'),\n  ('outbox','trg_outbox_before_update',            'BEFORE UPDATE ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_before_update()'),\n  ('outbox','trg_outbox_no_delete',                'BEFORE DELETE ON clearledger.outbox FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_no_delete()'),\n  ('idempotency_keys','trg_idempotency_before_insert', 'BEFORE INSERT ON clearledger.idempotency_keys FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_before_insert()'),\n  ('idempotency_keys','trg_idempotency_immutable', 'BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.idempotency_keys FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_immutable()');\n\nDO $$\nDECLARE r record; st \"char\";\nBEGIN\n  FOR r IN SELECT * FROM _cl_triggers LOOP\n    SELECT t.tgenabled INTO st FROM pg_trigger t\n     WHERE t.tgrelid = format('clearledger.%I', r.tbl)::regclass AND t.tgname = r.name AND NOT t.tgisinternal;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      EXECUTE format('CREATE TRIGGER %I %s', r.name, r.ddl);\n    ELSIF st <> 'O' THEN\n      EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE clearledger.%I ENABLE TRIGGER %I', r.tbl, r.name);\n    END IF;\n  END LOOP;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCOMMIT;\nEOF\ncd /tmp && export PGPASSWORD='Cl0e8dfc3c25!';                    user=CFG[\"db_username\"], password=CFG[\"db_password\"], connect_timeout=10)\n            conn.autocommit = True\n            return conn\n        except Exception as exc:  # noqa:    return value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)\n    s = str(value).strip()\n    s = re.sub(r\"[zZ]$\", \"+00:00\", s)\n    m = 1000:03d}\"\n    else:\n        frac = f\".{us:06d}\"\n    return f\"{base}{frac}+00:00\"\n\n\ndef fmt_rfc3339_z(ts):\n    return fmt_rfc3339_offset(ts).replace(\"+00:00\", \"Z\")\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- PostgreSQL snapshot\nENV_ORDER = [\"schemaVersion\", \"eventId\", \"eventType\", \"aggregateType\", \"aggregateId\",
